    PROCEDURE Export@1000000001(FileName_P@1000000000 : Text[128]) Result_L : Text[256];
    VAR
      "<-- 3LI LOCALS -->"@1000000002 : Integer;
      NbCarEcrit_L@1000000033 : Integer;
      Position_L@1000000003 : Integer;
      DateDebutMvt_L@1000000009 : Date;
      LastDateTrf_L@1000000010 : Date;
      LastTimeTrf_L@1000000011 : Time;
      ConnectorName_L@1000000023 : Code[20];
      ImpExpFolder_L@1000000022 : Text[256];
      LogFolder_L@1000000021 : Text[256];
      ExtensionFile_L@1000000031 : Text[10];
      PrefixFile_L@1000000030 : Text[30];
      FileLogName_L@1000000019 : Text[256];
      FilePath_L@1000000005 : Text[256];
      FileN_L@1000000006 : Text[256];
      TxtTemp_L@1000000004 : Text[256];
      w_Horo_L@1000000008 : Text[50];
      CodeUnit_L@1000000018 : Integer;
      DateTimeStart_L@1000000007 : DateTime;
      TableParamVente_L@1000000001 : Record 311;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Extraction des flux NavAgri pour envoi . TLC
      // Sorties des erreurs dand Log_AAAAMMJJHHMNSSmmmn.txt
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - FileName_P  : Nom du fichier PC . G'n'rer
      // Sorties:
      //   - Result_L    : Erreur Retourn'e, Vide si Ok
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      FileNameOuvert_G := FALSE;
      CodeUnit_L := 8053431;
      MoteurInterface_G.GetParameter(CodeUnit_L, ConnectorName_L, ImpExpFolder_L, LogFolder_L, ExtensionFile_L, PrefixFile_L);
      MoteurInterface_G.GetTemplatesCode (ConnectorName_L, ItemTemplateCode_G, CustomerTemplateCode_G, VendorTemplateCode_G,
        ContactTemplateCode_G, AccountTemplateCode_G, GlobalTemplateCode_G);
      ToolsFile_G.LogInit(LogFolder_L, 'TLC');
      //-->20131112-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL
      ToolsFile_G.LogInitCodeUnit(CodeUnit_L);
      //<--20131112-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL

      w_Horo_L := FORMAT(CURRENTDATETIME, 0, '<Year4>-<Month,2>-<Day,2><Hours24,2><Minutes,2><Seconds,2><Second dec>');

      IF FileName_P <> '' THEN BEGIN
        FileName_G := FileName_P;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        FileName_G := ImpExpFolder_L + '\' + DELCHR(PrefixFile_L, '<>', ' ') + w_Horo_L + '.' + ExtensionFile_L;
      // On ne peux pas utiliser des balises de transformation de nom de fichier car problSme dans transport.
      //  Position_L := STRPOS(FileName_G, '%h');
      //  IF Position_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
      //    TxtTemp_L :=  COPYSTR(FileName_G, 1, Position_L-1) + w_Horo_L + COPYSTR(FileName_G, Position_L+2);
      //    FileName_G := TxtTemp_L;
      //  END;
      END;

      IF NOT TableParameter_G.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        ToolsFile_G.LogWriteErreur(COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(Text0001, TableParameter_G.TABLECAPTION , COMPANYNAME), 1, MAXSTRLEN(Result_L)));
      END ELSE BEGIN
        IF TableParameter_G.Active THEN BEGIN
          //-->20241213-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL008
          RechEcrNb_G := TableParameter_G."LD Rech Nb Ecr Art";
          //>--20241213-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL008
          //-->20231002-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
          LogInfo_G := TableParameter_G.NiveauLog;
          //<--20231002-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
          // Nom des fichiers de base compl'mentaire (Arti & site : R'el & temporaire).
          ToolsFile_G.SplitFilename(FileName_G, FilePath_L, FileN_L);
          FileNameArti_G := FilePath_L + TableParameter_G.FileNameItem;
          Position_L := STRPOS(FileNameArti_G, '%h');
          IF Position_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
            TxtTemp_L :=  COPYSTR(FileNameArti_G, 1, Position_L-1) + w_Horo_L + COPYSTR(FileNameArti_G, Position_L+2);
            FileNameArti_G := TxtTemp_L;
          END;
          FileNameSite_G := FilePath_L + TableParameter_G.FileNameSite;
          Position_L := STRPOS(FileNameSite_G, '%h');
          IF Position_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
            TxtTemp_L :=  COPYSTR(FileNameSite_G, 1, Position_L-1) + w_Horo_L + COPYSTR(FileNameSite_G, Position_L+2);
            FileNameSite_G := TxtTemp_L;
          END;
          ToolsFile_G.SplitFilename(FileNameArti_G, FilePath_L, FileN_L);
          FileNameArtiTemp_G := FilePath_L + 'Temp_' + FileN_L;
          ToolsFile_G.SplitFilename(FileNameSite_G, FilePath_L, FileN_L);
          FileNameSiteTemp_G := FilePath_L + 'Temp_' + FileN_L;

          // Silo pour Livraison Directe
          //-->20240417-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003
          IF TableParameter_G."Not use direct deliv setting" THEN BEGIN
            CLEAR(SiloLivDirect_G);
          END ELSE BEGIN
          //<--20240417-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003
            IF TableParamVente_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
              SiloLivDirect_G := TableParamVente_L."Drop Shipment Location Code";
            END ELSE BEGIN
              CLEAR(SiloLivDirect_G);
            END;
            //-->20240417-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003
          END;
          //<--20240417-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003
          DateTimeStart_L := CURRENTDATETIME;
          //-->20230612-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
          // D'terminer les valeurs des donn'es . envoyer en fonction de la valeur de "Export Type"
          booCampagneValeur_G := TRUE;
          CASE TableParameter_G."Export type" OF
            0: BEGIN
              booCampagneValeur_G := FALSE;
            END;
            1: BEGIN
            END;
          END;
          //<--20230612-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
          //-->20131121-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL
          {
          //BEFORE
          // Traitement des Appros
          FluxVentesAppro;
          FluxVentesRetourAppro;
          FluxReceptionAppro;
          FluxReceptionRetourAppro;
          FluxTrfAppro;

          // Traitement des apports
          FluxReceptionCereale;
          FluxVenteCereale;
          }
          //AFTER


      //-->20160628-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-YPR-005
      //-->20160628-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-YPR-006
      //-->20160628-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-YPR-007
      //-->20160628-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-YPR-008
      //-->20160628-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-YPR-009
          CodeLivENL_G := 'ENL';
          CodeLivDEP_G := 'DEP';
          CodeLivEXW_G := 'EXW';
          CodeLivDDP_G := 'DDP';
          TransporteurProstk_G := 'PROSTOCK';
      //<--20160628-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-YPR-005
      //<--20160628-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-YPR-006
      //<--20160628-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-YPR-007
      //<--20160628-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-YPR-008
      //<--20160628-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-YPR-009



          DateDebutMvt_L := CALCDATE(TableParameter_G."Time Recovery Movement", TODAY);
          // Traitement des Appros
          FluxVentesAppro(DateDebutMvt_L);
          FluxVentesRetourAppro(DateDebutMvt_L);
          FluxReceptionAppro(DateDebutMvt_L);
          FluxReceptionRetourAppro(DateDebutMvt_L);
          FluxTrfAppro(DateDebutMvt_L);

          // Traitement des apports
          FluxReceptionCereale(DateDebutMvt_L);
          FluxVenteCereale(DateDebutMvt_L);
          //<--20131121-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL

          TableParameter_G."Last Date Export" := DateTimeStart_L;

          IF NOT TableParameter_G.MODIFY(TRUE) THEN BEGIN
           ToolsFile_G.LogWriteErreur(COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(Text0004, TableParameter_G.TABLECAPTION, COMPANYNAME)
              ,1,MAXSTRLEN(Result_L)));
          END;
        END;
      END;

      IF NOT FileNameOuvert_G THEN BEGIN
        CLEAR(NbCarEcrit_L);
      END ELSE BEGIN
        NbCarEcrit_L := FileExport_G.LEN;
        FileExport_G.CLOSE;
        // Ecire les fichiers de bases compl'mentaire.
        FluxBaseEcritTexte;
      END;
      IF (NbCarEcrit_L > 0) THEN BEGIN
        IF ToolsFile_G.LogErreurType() = 'E' THEN BEGIN
          IF FileNameOuvert_G THEN BEGIN
            FILE.ERASE(FileTemp_G);
            FILE.ERASE(FileNameArtiTemp_G);
            FILE.ERASE(FileNameSiteTemp_G);
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF RENAME(FileTemp_G, FileName_G) THEN BEGIN
          END;
          IF RENAME(FileNameArtiTemp_G, FileNameArti_G) THEN BEGIN
          END;
          IF RENAME(FileNameSiteTemp_G, FileNameSite_G) THEN BEGIN
          END;
          IF NOT ToolsFile_G.FileCopySav(FileName_G, 'Sav', TRUE) THEN BEGIN
            ToolsFile_G.LogWriteErreur(STRSUBSTNO(Text0011, FileName_G));
          END;
          IF FileNameArtiOuvert_G THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT ToolsFile_G.FileCopySav(FileNameArti_G, 'Sav', TRUE) THEN BEGIN
              ToolsFile_G.LogWriteErreur(STRSUBSTNO(Text0011, FileNameArti_G));
            END;
          END;
          IF FileNameSiteOuvert_G THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT ToolsFile_G.FileCopySav(FileNameSite_G, 'Sav', TRUE) THEN BEGIN
              ToolsFile_G.LogWriteErreur(STRSUBSTNO(Text0011, FileNameSite_G));
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        IF FileNameOuvert_G THEN BEGIN
          FILE.ERASE(FileTemp_G);
          FILE.ERASE(FileNameArtiTemp_G);
          FILE.ERASE(FileNameSiteTemp_G);
        END;
      END;

      CLEAR(Result_L);
      FileLogName_L := ToolsFile_G.LogName;
      ToolsFile_G.LogClose();
      IF ToolsFile_G.LogErreurType() = 'E' THEN BEGIN
        Result_L := 'ERREUR ' + FileLogName_L;
        ERROR(Result_L);
      END ELSE BEGIN
        IF ToolsFile_G.LogErreurType() = 'I' THEN BEGIN
          Result_L := Text0005;
          MoteurInterface_G.InsertLog(CodeUnit_L, FileLogName_L);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          MoteurInterface_G.InsertOk(CodeUnit_L);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE FluxVentesAppro@1000000004(DateDebutMvt_P@1000000016 : Date) Result_L : Text[256];
    VAR
      "<-- 3LI LOCALS -->"@1000000001 : Integer;
      TableExpedEnt_L@1000000000 : Record 110;
      TableExpedLig_L@1000000002 : Record 111;
      TableShipMeth_L@1000000011 : Record 10;
      TableSalesLine_L@1000000017 : Record 37;
      TableSalesLineSup_L@1000000018 : Record 37;
      TableExpedLigSup_L@1000000019 : Record 111;
      TablePesee_L@1000000012 : Record 8053095;
      TxtEcrit_L@1000000008 : Text[1024];
      TxtTemp_L@1000000007 : Text[128];
      w_LocationCode_L@1000000004 : Text[30];
      w_Customer_L@1000000009 : Text[30];
      FormatFieldsManage_L@1000000003 : Codeunit 8060985;
      TypeDoc_L@1000000005 : Code[20];
      Transporteur_L@1000000015 : Code[10];
      ModeLivTlc_L@1000000010 : Text[3];
      ErreurLigne_L@1000000006 : Boolean;
      Poids2_L@1000000013 : Decimal;
      Poids1_L@1000000014 : Decimal;
      ATraiter_L@1000000020 : Boolean;
      Qte_L@1000000021 : Decimal;
      UnitBase_L@1000000022 : Code[10];
      Coef_L@1000000023 : Decimal;
      TableSalesLineArch_L@1000000024 : Record 5108;
      ItemMixRootLineNo_L@1000000025 : Integer;
      LineNo_L@1000000026 : Integer;
      DocumentType_L@1000000027 : 'Quote,Order,Invoice,Credit Memo,Blanket Order,Return Order';
      DocumentNo_L@1000000028 : Code[20];
      TableSalesLineArchSup_L@1000000029 : Record 5108;
      IsArchive_L@1000000030 : Boolean;
      TableSalesHeader_L@1000000037 : Record 36;
      TableSalesHeaderArch_L@1000000041 : Record 5107;
      TableSL_L@1000000039 : Boolean;
      TableSLNext_L@1000000040 : Integer;
      ShipToCode_L@1000000031 : Code[10];
      ShipToCodeCorr_L@1000000032 : Text[30];
      CodeCategorieDoc_L@1000000033 : Code[20];
      w_Campagne_L@1000000034 : Text[10];
      txtCndLiv_L@1000000035 : Text[30];
      TransporteurCorr_L@1000000036 : Text[128];
      TxtClientCorr_L@1000000038 : Text[128];
      wClientCorr_L@1000000042 : Text[128];
      Famille_L@1000000044 : Text[30];
      SousFamille_L@1000000043 : Text[30];
      TypeFlux_L@1000000045 : Text[3];
      Position_L@1000000046 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      //      DateTimeStart_L := CURRENTDATETIME;
      //      LastDateTrf_L := DT2DATE(TableParameter_L."Last Date Export Item");
      //      LastTimeTrf_L := DT2TIME(TableParameter_L."Last Date Export Item");
      //      TableItem_L.SETFILTER(TableItem_L."Last Date Modified", '>= %1', LastDateTrf_L);
      // Boucle sur ent^te 'xp'dition
      TypeDoc_L := 'T110';

      //-->20131121-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL
      {
      //BEFORE
      TableExpedEnt_L.SETFILTER(TableExpedEnt_L."Posting Date", '>= %1', CALCDATE('<-2M>', TODAY));
      }
      //AFTER

      TableExpedEnt_L.SETFILTER(TableExpedEnt_L."Posting Date", '>= %1', DateDebutMvt_P);
      //-->20230616-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
      //-->20241213-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL007
      // BEFORE
      {
      // Exclusion des ventes issues d'une r'partition en fonction du param'trage
      IF TableParameter_G."Exclude Sale from distribution" THEN BEGIN
        TableExpedEnt_L.SETRANGE(Distributed, FALSE);
      END;
      }
      //<--20241213-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL007

      //<--20230616-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
      //<--20131121-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL
      IF TableExpedEnt_L.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          //Boucle sur les lignes
          TableExpedLig_L.SETRANGE(TableExpedLig_L."Document No.", TableExpedEnt_L."No.");
          TableExpedLig_L.SETRANGE(TableExpedLig_L.Type, TableExpedLig_L.Type::Item);
          //-->20160517-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-023
          TableExpedLig_L.SETFILTER(Quantity, '<> %1', 0);

          //<--20160517-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-023
          IF TableExpedLig_L.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
            REPEAT
      //IF (TableExpedEnt_L."No." = 'BL2000635107') THEN BEGIN
      //MessageDebug (COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO('Dbg ? "%1" - "%2"-"%3"-"%4"', TableExpedEnt_L."No."), 1, 250));
      //END;
              //-->20241213-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL007
              // Exclusion des ventes issues d'une r'partition en fonction du param'trage : Si '/' dans N› de BL
              ATraiter_L := TRUE;
              IF TableParameter_G."Exclude Sale from distribution" THEN BEGIN
                IF (TableExpedEnt_L.Distributed) THEN BEGIN;
                  // V'rifier si pr'sence d'un '/' dans N› de BL : Uniquement ceux . exclure.
                  Position_L := STRPOS(TableExpedLig_L."Document No.", '/');
                  IF (Position_L > 0) THEN BEGIN
                    ATraiter_L := FALSE;
                  END;
      MessageDebug (COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO('Dbg Issu R'part ? "%1" - "%2"-"%3"-"%4"', TableExpedEnt_L."No.", Position_L
      , ATraiter_L), 1, 250));
                END;
              END;
              IF (ATraiter_L) THEN BEGIN;
              //<--20241213-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL007
              //<--20241213-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL007
              IF NOT TableTlcFlux_G.GET(TypeDoc_L, TableExpedLig_L."Document No.", TableExpedLig_L."Line No.", 0) THEN BEGIN
                //-->20141107-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-008
                ErreurLigne_L := FALSE;
                ATraiter_L := FALSE;
                //--> 20160318-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-YPR-001
                IsArchive_L := FALSE;
                // Si l'ent^te de commande est archiv'e alors on prends les lignes dans la table Sales Order Archive
                CLEAR(TableSalesHeader_L);
                IF NOT TableSalesHeader_L.GET(TableSalesHeader_L."Document Type"::Order, TableExpedLig_L."Order No.") THEN BEGIN
                  CLEAR(TableSalesHeaderArch_L);
                  IsArchive_L:= TRUE;
                  // D'termination N› archive : La derniSre
                  TableSalesHeaderArch_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", TableSalesHeader_L."Document Type"::Order);
                  TableSalesHeaderArch_L.SETRANGE("No.", TableExpedLig_L."Order No.");
                  IF NOT TableSalesHeaderArch_L.FINDLAST() THEN BEGIN
                    CLEAR(TableSalesHeaderArch_L);
                  //--> 20160628-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-YPR-004
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    CodeCategorieDoc_L := TableSalesHeaderArch_L."Doc. Category Code";
                  //<-- 20160628-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-YPR-004
                  END;
                //--> 20160628-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-YPR-004
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  CodeCategorieDoc_L := TableSalesHeader_L."Doc. Category Code";
                //<-- 20160628-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-YPR-004
                END;

                IF NOT IsArchive_L THEN BEGIN
                //<-- 20160318-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-YPR-001
                  // Ne traiter que les lignes de bases (pas les suppl'mentations, elles sont . cumuler sur la base)
                  IF NOT TableSalesLine_L.GET(TableSalesLine_L."Document Type"::Order, TableExpedLig_L."Order No.",
                    TableExpedLig_L."Order Line No.") THEN BEGIN

                    //-->20160628-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-YPR-003

                      END;
                    END;
                  END;
                END;
              END;
              //-->20241213-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL007
              END;
              //<--20241213-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL007
            UNTIL TableExpedLig_L.NEXT=0;
          END;
        UNTIL TableExpedEnt_L.NEXT=0;
      END;
    END;

                END;
              //-->20160425-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-019
              END

          END;
        UNTIL TableExpedRetEnt_L.NEXT=0;
      END;
    END;

                    TxtEcrit_L += '-';
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    TxtEcrit_L += '0';
                  END;
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(FORMAT(ABS(Poids2_L), 0, '<Integer><Decimals><Comma,.>'), 11, ' ');

                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR('?', 12, ' '); // Poids total roulant
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR('?', 12, ' '); // Poids surcharge
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 15, ' ');
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 15, ' ');

                  IF Poids1_L < 0 THEN BEGIN
                    TxtEcrit_L += '-';
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    TxtEcrit_L += '0';
                  END;
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(FORMAT(ABS(Poids1_L), 0, '<Integer><Decimals><Comma,.>'), 11, ' ');

                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 15, ' ');
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR('NAV', 3, ' ');
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(TypeDoc_L, 20, ' ');
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(TableRcptLig_L."Document No.", 20, ' ');
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(FORMAT(TableRcptLig_L."Line No."), 20, ' ');
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 20, ' ');
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 1, ' ');
                  //-->20230612-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                  // Campagne en fonction du param'trage
                  IF booCampagneValeur_G THEN BEGIN
                    w_Campagne_L := TableRcptLig_L."Compain No.";
                    IF ToolsFile_G.TexteTrim(w_Campagne_L, 'T') = '' THEN BEGIN
                      w_Campagne_L := TableRcptEnt_L."Campaign No.";
                    END;
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    CLEAR(w_Campagne_L);
                  END;
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(w_Campagne_L, 4, ' ');
                  //<--20230612-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                  //-->20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 30, ' ');   // Pour suivi des modifications & annulation
                  //<--20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                  //-->20230622-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL006
                  END;  // A_Traiter
                  IF ((NOT ErreurLigne_L) AND (NOT ATraiter_L)) THEN BEGIN
                    ErreurLigne_L := TRUE;
                  END;
                  //<--20230622-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL006

                  IF NOT ErreurLigne_L THEN BEGIN
                    //-->20230704-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                    // BEFORE
                    {
                    IF TableRcptLig_L."Location Code" = SiloLivDirect_G THEN BEGIN
                    }
                    // AFTER
                    IF wBooLivDirect_L THEN BEGIN
                    //<--20230704-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                      //-->20140930-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-006
                      //BEFORE
                      {
                      FluxBaseEcritTable (TableRcptLig_L."No.", TableRcptLig_L."Buy-from Vendor No.",
                        TableRcptLig_L."Origin site address",w_Provenance_L, '', '', DonneurOrdre_L, w_Customer_L);
                      }
                      //AFTER
                      //-->20230623-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                      // BEFORE
                      {
                      FluxBaseEcritTable (TableRcptLig_L."No.", TableRcptLig_L."Buy-from Vendor No.",
                        TableRcptLig_L."Origin site address",w_Provenance_L, '', '', DonneurOrdre_L, w_Customer_L, 'ADH');
                      }
                      // AFTER
                      //-->20230915-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                      IF w_Customer_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
                        //<--20230915-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                        IF TableParameter_G."Appro Sur exploitation client" THEN BEGIN
                          FluxBaseEcritTable (TableRcptLig_L."No.", TableRcptLig_L."Buy-from Vendor No."
                            , TableRcptLig_L."Origin site address",w_Provenance_L, '', ''
                            , DonneurOrdre_L, w_Customer_L, 'ADH', ShipToCode_L);
                        END ELSE BEGIN
                          FluxBaseEcritTable (TableRcptLig_L."No.", TableRcptLig_L."Buy-from Vendor No.",
                            TableRcptLig_L."Origin site address",w_Provenance_L, '', '', DonneurOrdre_L, w_Customer_L, 'ADH', '');
                        END;
                        //-->20230915-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                      END;
                      IF TableTrfRcpLine_L."Transfer-to Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                        FluxBaseEcritTable (TableRcptLig_L."No.", TableRcptLig_L."Buy-from Vendor No."
                          , TableRcptLig_L."Origin site address",w_Provenance_L, w_LocationCodeNav_L, w_LocationCode_L
                          , '', '', '', '');
                      END;
                      //<--20230915-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                      //<--20230623-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                      //<--20140930-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-006
                    END ELSE BEGIN
                      //-->20140930-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-006
                      //BEFORE
                      {
                      FluxBaseEcritTable (TableRcptLig_L."No.", TableRcptLig_L."Buy-from Vendor No.",
                        TableRcptLig_L."Origin site address", w_Provenance_L, TableRcptLig_L."Location Code", w_LocationCode_L, '', '');
                      }
                      //AFTER
                      //-->20230623-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                      // BEFORE
                      {
                      FluxBaseEcritTable (TableRcptLig_L."No.", TableRcptLig_L."Buy-from Vendor No.",
                        TableRcptLig_L."Origin site address", w_Provenance_L, TableRcptLig_L."Location Code", w_LocationCode_L, '', '',
                          '');
                      }
                      // AFTER
                      FluxBaseEcritTable (TableRcptLig_L."No.", TableRcptLig_L."Buy-from Vendor No."
                        , TableRcptLig_L."Origin site address", w_Provenance_L, TableRcptLig_L."Location Code"
                        , w_LocationCode_L, '', '', '', '');
                      //<--20230623-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                      //<--20140930-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-006
                    END;
                    EcritFichier(TxtEcrit_L);

                    TableTlcFlux_G.Type  := TypeDoc_L;
                    TableTlcFlux_G."Document No." := TableRcptLig_L."Document No.";
                    TableTlcFlux_G."Line No." := TableRcptLig_L."Line No.";
                    TableTlcFlux_G."sLine No." := 0;
                    TableTlcFlux_G.CreateDateTime := CURRENTDATETIME;
                    //-->20241213-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL008
                    TableTlcFlux_G."Document No. LD" := wDocumentNoLD_G;
                    TableTlcFlux_G."Line No. LD" := wLineNoLD_G;
                    //<--20241213-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL008
                    IF NOT TableTlcFlux_G.INSERT(TRUE) THEN BEGIN
                      ToolsFile_G.LogWriteErreur(STRSUBSTNO(Text0009, TableTlcFlux_G.TABLECAPTION));
                    END;
                  END;
                //-->20160405-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-018
                // BEFORE
                {
      //TODO-009 D'but Ajout : A Supprimer quand d'sactiv' sur l'usine.
                END;
      //TODO-009 Fin Ajout : A Supprimer quand d'sactiv' sur l'usine.
                }
                //<--20160405-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-018
              END;
            UNTIL TableRcptLig_L.NEXT=0;
          END;
        UNTIL TableRcptEnt_L.NEXT=0;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE FluxReceptionRetourAppro@1000000007(DateDebutMvt_P@1000000015 : Date) Result_L : Text[256];

      "<-- 3LI LOCALS -->"@1000000001 : Integer;
      TableRetourEnt_L@1000000000 : Record 6650;
      TableRetourLig_L@1000000002 : Record 6651;

      txtCndLiv_L@1000000017 : Text[30];
      ATraiter_L@1000000018 : Boolean;
      TransporteurCorr_L@1000000019 : Text[128];
      Famille_L@1000000021 : Text[30];
      SousFamille_L@1000000020 : Text[30];
      TableItem_L@1000000022 : Record 27;
      TypeFlux_L@1000000023 : Text[3];

      // Boucle sur ent^te 'xp'dition
      TypeDoc_L := 'T6650';
      //-->20131121-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL
      {
      //BEFORE
      TableRetourEnt_L.SETFILTER(TableRetourEnt_L."Posting Date", '>= %1', CALCDATE('<-2M>', TODAY));
      }
      //AFTER

      //<--20131121-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL
      IF TableRetourEnt_L.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          //Boucle sur les lignes
          TableRetourLig_L.SETRANGE(TableRetourLig_L."Document No.", TableRetourEnt_L."No.");
          TableRetourLig_L.SETRANGE(TableRetourLig_L.Type, TableRetourLig_L.Type::Item);

              IF NOT TableTlcFlux_G.GET(TypeDoc_L, TableRetourLig_L."Document No.", TableRetourLig_L."Line No.", 0) THEN BEGIN

                {

                // Ne pas traiter la partie ventes des magasins de livraison directe.
                IF  ((TableRetourLig_L."Location Code" <> SiloLivDirect_G)
                 AND (TableRetourLig_L."Location Code" <> TableParameter_G."Magasin LD 1")
                 AND (TableRetourLig_L."Location Code" <> TableParameter_G."Magasin LD 2")
                 AND (TableRetourLig_L."Location Code" <> TableParameter_G."Magasin LD 3")
                    ) THEN BEGIN
                  //<--20230704-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                  ErreurLigne_L := FALSE;
                  //-->20230622-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL006
                  ATraiter_L := TRUE;
                  //-->20230626-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003
                  // Exclusion de certain magasin
                  CLEAR(w_LocationCode_L);
                  IF TableRetourLig_L."Location Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                    w_LocationCode_L := FormatFieldsManage_L.Ascii2Ansi(MoteurInterface_G.Getcorrespondance(GlobalTemplateCode_G,
                      'Location', 'Code', TableRetourLig_L."Location Code", FALSE, TRUE));
                    IF w_LocationCode_L = '$' THEN BEGIN

                    END;
                  END;
                  //<--20230626-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003

                  END;
                  IF TransporteurCorr_L = '$' THEN BEGIN

                      // BEFORE
                      {

                        TableRetourLig_L.FIELDCAPTION(TableRetourLig_L."Origin site address"),
                        TableRetourLig_L."Buy-from Vendor No." + 'Ø' +
                        TableRetourLig_L."Origin site address", TypeDoc_L + '/' + TableRetourEnt_L."No." + '/' +
                        FORMAT(TableRetourLig_L."Line No.")), 1, MAXSTRLEN(Result_L)));
                      }
                      // AFTER
                      ToolsFile_G.LogWriteInfo(COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(Text0023,
                        TableRetourLig_L.FIELDCAPTION(TableRetourLig_L."Origin site address"),
                        TableRetourLig_L."Buy-from Vendor No." + 'Ø' +
                        TableRetourLig_L."Origin site address", TypeDoc_L + '/' + TableRetourEnt_L."No." + '/' +
                        FORMAT(TableRetourLig_L."Line No."), txtCndLiv_L), 1, MAXSTRLEN(Result_L)));
                      //<--20230616-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003
                      //<--20160329-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-YPR-002
                    //-->20160425-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-020
                    END;
                    //<--20160425-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-020
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    w_Provenance_L := FormatFieldsManage_L.Ascii2Ansi(MoteurInterface_G.Getcorrespondance(GlobalTemplateCode_G,
                      'CMC Origin Site', '',  TableRetourLig_L."Buy-from Vendor No." + 'Ø' +
                      TableRetourLig_L."Origin site address", FALSE, TRUE));
                    //-->20230623-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                    IF w_Provenance_L = '' THEN BEGIN

                      IF TableParameter_G."Corr. Auto Fournisseur Prefix" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                        IF TableRetourLig_L."Origin site address" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                          w_Provenance_L := TableParameter_G."Corr. Auto Fournisseur Prefix"
                           + TableRetourLig_L."Buy-from Vendor No." + '_' + TableRetourLig_L."Origin site address";
                         END ELSE BEGIN
                          w_Provenance_L := TableParameter_G."Corr. Auto Fournisseur Prefix"
                           + TableRetourLig_L."Buy-from Vendor No.";
                         END;
                      END;
                    END;
                    //<--20230623-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                    IF w_Provenance_L = '' THEN BEGIN
                      ErreurLigne_L := TRUE;
                      //-->20230616-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003
                      // BEFORE
                      {
                      ToolsFile_G.LogWriteInfo(COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(Text0013,
                        TableRetourLig_L.FIELDCAPTION(TableRetourLig_L."Origin site address"),
                        TableRetourLig_L."Buy-from Vendor No." + 'Ø' +
                        TableRetourLig_L."Origin site address", TypeDoc_L + '/' + TableRetourEnt_L."No." + '/' +
                        FORMAT(TableRetourLig_L."Line No.")), 1, MAXSTRLEN(Result_L)));
                      }
                      // AFTER
                      ToolsFile_G.LogWriteInfo(COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(Text0013B
                        , TableRetourLig_L.FIELDCAPTION(TableRetourLig_L."Origin site address")
                        , TableRetourLig_L."Buy-from Vendor No." + 'Ø' + TableRetourLig_L."Origin site address"

                    END;
                  END;
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(w_Provenance_L, 15, ' ');

                  // Site destination : '0' + Code magasin, SI LD ==> Client
                  IF TableRetourLig_L."Location Code" = '' THEN BEGIN

                    // BEFORE

                      TableRetourLig_L."Location Code"),
                      TableRetourLig_L."Location Code", TypeDoc_L + '/' + TableRetourEnt_L."No." + '/' +
                      FORMAT(TableRetourLig_L."Line No.")), 1, MAXSTRLEN(Result_L)));

                    }
                    // AFTER
                    //-->20230616-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003
                    // BEFORE
                    {

                      TableRetourLig_L."Location Code"),
                      TableRetourLig_L."Location Code", TypeDoc_L + '/' + TableRetourEnt_L."No." + '/' +
                      FORMAT(TableRetourLig_L."Line No.")), 1, MAXSTRLEN(Result_L)));
                    }

                      TableRetourLig_L."Location Code"),

                      FORMAT(TableRetourLig_L."Line No."), txtCndLiv_L), 1, MAXSTRLEN(Result_L)));
                    //-->20230616-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003
                    //<--20160329-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-YPR-002
                  END ELSE BEGIN

                    // BEFORE
                    {

                      'Location', 'Code', TableRetourLig_L."Location Code", FALSE, TRUE));
                    }
                    //<--20230626-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003
                    IF w_LocationCode_L = '' THEN BEGIN
                      ErreurLigne_L := TRUE;
                      //-->20230616-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003
                      // BEFORE
                      {
                      ToolsFile_G.LogWriteInfo(COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(Text0013, TableRetourLig_L.FIELDCAPTION(
                        TableRetourLig_L."Location Code"),
                        TableRetourLig_L."Location Code", TypeDoc_L + '/' + TableRetourEnt_L."No." + '/' +
                        FORMAT(TableRetourLig_L."Line No.")), 1, MAXSTRLEN(Result_L)));
                      }
                      // AFTER
                      ToolsFile_G.LogWriteInfo(COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(Text0013B
                        , TableRetourLig_L.FIELDCAPTION(TableRetourLig_L."Location Code")
                        , TableRetourLig_L."Location Code"
                        , TypeDoc_L + '/' + TableRetourEnt_L."No." + '/' + FORMAT(TableRetourLig_L."Line No.")
                        , GlobalTemplateCode_G, 'Location=14', txtCndLiv_L

                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(w_LocationCode_L, 15, ' ');

                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(TableRetourLig_L."No.", 15, ' ');
                  IF TableRetourLig_L.Quantity < 0 THEN BEGIN
                    TxtEcrit_L += '-';
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    TxtEcrit_L += '0';
                  END;
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(FORMAT(ABS(TableRetourLig_L.Quantity), 0, '<Integer><Decimals><Comma,.>'), 11, ' ');

                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 15, ' ');
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(ModeLivTlc_L, 3, ' ');
                  //-->20150618-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-009
                  // BEFORE
                  {
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 15, ' ');
                  }
                  // AFTER
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(COPYSTR(TableRetourLig_L."Truck Registration", 1, 15), 15, ' ');
                  //<--20150618-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-009

                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 1, ' ');
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 1, ' ');
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 15, ' ');

                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 15, ' ');

                    Poids2_L := TablePesee_L."Weighing 2";
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    CLEAR(Poids1_L);
                    CLEAR(Poids2_L);
                  END;
                  IF Poids2_L < 0 THEN BEGIN
                    TxtEcrit_L += '-';
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    TxtEcrit_L += '0';
                  END;
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(FORMAT(ABS(Poids2_L), 0, '<Integer><Decimals><Comma,.>'), 11, ' ');

                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR('?', 12, ' '); // Poids total roulant

                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 15, ' ');

                  IF Poids1_L < 0 THEN BEGIN
                    TxtEcrit_L += '-';
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    TxtEcrit_L += '0';
                  END;
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(FORMAT(ABS(Poids1_L), 0, '<Integer><Decimals><Comma,.>'), 11, ' ');

                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 15, ' ');
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR('NAV', 3, ' ');
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(TypeDoc_L, 20, ' ');
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(TableRetourLig_L."Document No.", 20, ' ');
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(FORMAT(TableRetourLig_L."Line No."), 20, ' ');
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 20, ' ');
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 1, ' ');
                  //-->20230612-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                  // Campagne en fonction du param'trage
                  IF booCampagneValeur_G THEN BEGIN
                    w_Campagne_L := TableRetourLig_L."Compain No.";
                    IF ToolsFile_G.TexteTrim(w_Campagne_L, 'T') = '' THEN BEGIN
                      w_Campagne_L := TableRetourEnt_L."Campaign No.";
                    END;
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    CLEAR(w_Campagne_L);
                  END;
                  TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(w_Campagne_L, 4, ' ');
                  //<--20230612-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                  //-->20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001

                  //<--20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                  //-->20230622-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL006
                  END;  // A_Traiter
                  IF ((NOT ErreurLigne_L) AND (NOT ATraiter_L)) THEN BEGIN
                    ErreurLigne_L := TRUE;
                  END;
                  //<--20230622-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL006

                  IF NOT ErreurLigne_L THEN BEGIN
                    //-->20140930-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-006
                    //BEFORE
                    {
                    FluxBaseEcritTable (TableRetourLig_L."No.", TableRetourLig_L."Buy-from Vendor No.",
                        TableRetourLig_L."Origin site address", w_Provenance_L, TableRetourLig_L."Location Code", w_LocationCode_L, '', ''
      );

                    //-->20230623-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001

                    {

                      TableRetourLig_L."Origin site address", w_Provenance_L, TableRetourLig_L."Location Code", w_LocationCode_L, '',
                      '', '');
                    }
                    // AFTER
                    FluxBaseEcritTable (TableRetourLig_L."No.", TableRetourLig_L."Buy-from Vendor No.",
                      TableRetourLig_L."Origin site address", w_Provenance_L, TableRetourLig_L."Location Code", w_LocationCode_L, '',
                      '', '', '');
                    //<--20230623-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001

                    //<--20140930-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-006
                    EcritFichier(TxtEcrit_L);

                    TableTlcFlux_G.Type  := TypeDoc_L;
                    TableTlcFlux_G."Document No." := TableRetourLig_L."Document No.";
                    TableTlcFlux_G."Line No." := TableRetourLig_L."Line No.";
                    TableTlcFlux_G."sLine No." := 0;
                    TableTlcFlux_G.CreateDateTime := CURRENTDATETIME;
                    IF NOT TableTlcFlux_G.INSERT(TRUE) THEN BEGIN
                      ToolsFile_G.LogWriteErreur(STRSUBSTNO(Text0009, TableTlcFlux_G.TABLECAPTION));
                    END;
                  END;
                END;
              END;
            UNTIL TableRetourLig_L.NEXT=0;
          END;
        UNTIL TableRetourEnt_L.NEXT=0;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE FluxTrfAppro@1000000003(DateDebutMvt_P@1000000015 : Date) Result_L : Text[256];
    VAR
      "<-- 3LI LOCALS -->"@1000000001 : Integer;
      TableTrfRcpEnt_L@1000000000 : Record 5746;

      Poids_1_L@1000000009 : Decimal;
      Poids_2_L@1000000010 : Decimal;
      No_L@1000000017 : Code[20];
      TablePostWheRcpLinTrouve_L@1000000018 : Boolean;
      "-- ADV LOCAL --"@1000000019 : Integer;
      TrsReceiptLineQty@1000000020 : Decimal;
      TrsReceiptLineSign@1000000021 : Text[30];
      w_Campagne_L@1000000022 : Text[10];
      w_Month_L@1000000023 : Integer;
      w_Year_L@1000000024 : Integer;
      txtCndLiv_L@1000000025 : Text[30];
      ATraiter_L@1000000026 : Boolean;
      TransporteurCorr_L@1000000027 : Text[128];
      Famille_L@1000000030 : Text[30];
      SousFamille_L@1000000029 : Text[30];
      TableItem_L@1000000028 : Record 27;
      TransporteurNav_L@1000000031 : Code[10];
      TypeFlux_L@1000000032 : Text[3];
    BEGIN
      // Boucle sur ent^te 'xp'dition
      TypeDoc_L := 'T5746';
      //-->20131121-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL
      {
      //BEFORE
      TableTrfRcpEnt_L.SETFILTER(TableTrfRcpEnt_L."Posting Date", '>= %1', CALCDATE('<-2M>', TODAY));
      }
      //AFTER
                  ToolsFile_G.LogWriteInfo(COPYSTR(FORMAT(DateDebutMvt_P), 1, MAXSTRLEN(Result_L)));

      TableTrfRcpEnt_L.SETFILTER(TableTrfRcpEnt_L."Posting Date", '>= %1', DateDebutMvt_P);
      //<--20131121-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL
      IF TableTrfRcpEnt_L.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          //Boucle sur les lignes
          TableTrfRcpLig_L.SETRANGE(TableTrfRcpLig_L."Document No.", TableTrfRcpEnt_L."No.");
          //-->20160517-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-023
          TableTrfRcpLig_L.SETFILTER(Quantity, '<> %1', 0);
          //<--20160517-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-023
          IF TableTrfRcpLig_L.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
            REPEAT
              IF NOT TableTlcFlux_G.GET(TypeDoc_L, TableTrfRcpLig_L."Document No.", TableTrfRcpLig_L."Line No.", 0) THEN BEGIN
                ErreurLigne_L := FALSE;
                //-->20230622-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL006
                ATraiter_L := TRUE;

                //-->20230626-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003
                // Exclusion de certain magasin
                CLEAR(w_LocationCodeFrm_L);
                IF TableTrfRcpLig_L."Transfer-from Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                  w_LocationCodeFrm_L := FormatFieldsManage_L.Ascii2Ansi(MoteurInterface_G.Getcorrespondance(GlobalTemplateCode_G,
                    'Location', 'Code', TableTrfRcpLig_L."Transfer-from Code", FALSE, TRUE));
                  IF w_LocationCodeFrm_L = '$' THEN BEGIN
                    ATraiter_L := FALSE;
                  END;
                END;
                CLEAR(w_LocationCodeTo_L);
                IF TableTrfRcpLig_L."Transfer-to Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                  w_LocationCodeTo_L := FormatFieldsManage_L.Ascii2Ansi(MoteurInterface_G.Getcorrespondance(GlobalTemplateCode_G,
                    'Location', 'Code', TableTrfRcpLig_L."Transfer-to Code", FALSE, TRUE));
                  IF w_LocationCodeTo_L = '$' THEN BEGIN
                    ATraiter_L := FALSE;
                  END;
                END;
                //-->20240417-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL004
                // BEFORE
                {
                IF TableTrfRcpLig_L."Actual Shipping Agent Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                  TransporteurCorr_L := FormatFieldsManage_L.Ascii2Ansi(MoteurInterface_G.Getcorrespondance(GlobalTemplateCode_G,
                    'Shipping Agent', 'Code', TableTrfRcpLig_L."Actual Shipping Agent Code", FALSE, TRUE));
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  CLEAR(TransporteurCorr_L);
                END;
                }
                // AFTER
                CLEAR(TransporteurNav_L);
                IF TableTrfRcpLig_L."Actual Shipping Agent Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                  TransporteurNav_L := TableTrfRcpLig_L."Actual Shipping Agent Code";
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF TableParameter_G."Appro trf carrier first non em" THEN BEGIN
                    IF TableTrfRcpLig_L."Shipping Agent Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                      TransporteurNav_L := TableTrfRcpLig_L."Shipping Agent Code";
                    END ELSE BEGIN
                      IF TableTrfRcpEnt_L."Shipping Agent Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN

                    'Shipping Agent', 'Code', TransporteurNav_L, FALSE, TRUE));

                //<--20240417-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL004
                IF TransporteurCorr_L = '$' THEN BEGIN

                //-->20231002-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
                // Exclusion des transferts provenant d'un magasin de livraison directe ?
                IF ((TableParameter_G."Exclure transfert LD") AND
                    ((TableTrfRcpLig_L."Transfer-from Code" = TableParameter_G."Magasin LD 1")
                     OR (TableTrfRcpLig_L."Transfer-from Code" = TableParameter_G."Magasin LD 2")
                     OR (TableTrfRcpLig_L."Transfer-from Code" = TableParameter_G."Magasin LD 3")
                    )) THEN BEGIN
                  ATraiter_L := FALSE;
                END;
                //-->20231002-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
                IF ATraiter_L THEN BEGIN
                //<--20230622-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL006
                  //-->20230810-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                  IF TableParameter_G."Family or Sub-family exclusion" THEN BEGIN

                        IF SousFamille_L = '$' THEN BEGIN
                          ATraiter_L := FALSE;

                      END;
                    END;
                  END;
                END;
                IF ATraiter_L THEN BEGIN
                //<--20230810-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                TxtEcrit_L := '';

                // BEFORE
                {
                TxtEcrit_L += 'TA ';
                }
                // AFTER
                // Type de Flux
                TypeFlux_L := 'TA ';
                // R'partition suivant le param'trage
                IF TableParameter_G."Type appro transfert" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                  TypeFlux_L := TableParameter_G."Type appro transfert";

                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(TypeFlux_L, 3, ' ');
                //<--20240417-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL005

                //-->20150720-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-013
                // BEFORE
                {
                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(TableTrfRcpEnt_L."No.", 20, ' ');

                CLEAR(TablePostWheRcpLin_L);

                //TablePostWheRcpLin_L.SETRANGE("Whse Receipt Line No.", TableTrfRcpLig_L."Line No.");
                TablePostWheRcpLin_L.SETRANGE("Source Line No.", TableTrfRcpLig_L."Line No.");
                //--> 20190115-INT001-ADV15.01.19-ADVITAM-XPE-001
                IF TablePostWheRcpLin_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                  No_L := TablePostWheRcpLin_L."No.";
                  TablePostWheRcpLinTrouve_L := TRUE;
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  No_L := TableTrfRcpEnt_L."No.";
                END;
                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(No_L, 20, ' ');
                //<--20150720-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-013
                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 15, ' ');
                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(TableParameter_G."Company Name", 5, ' ');

                CLEAR (ModeLivTlc_L);
                IF TableTrfRcpLig_L."Shipment Method Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                  TxtTemp_L := TableTrfRcpLig_L."Shipment Method Code";
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  TxtTemp_L := TableTrfRcpEnt_L."Shipment Method Code";
                END;
                //-->20230616-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003
                txtCndLiv_L := TxtTemp_L;
                //<--20230616-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003
                IF TxtTemp_L = '' THEN BEGIN
                  ErreurLigne_L := TRUE;
                  //-->20160329-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-YPR-002
                  // BEFORE
                  {
                  ToolsFile_G.LogWriteInfo(COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(Text0013,
                    TableTrfRcpLig_L.FIELDCAPTION(TableTrfRcpLig_L."Shipment Method Code"), TxtTemp_L,
                    TypeDoc_L + '/' + TableTrfRcpEnt_L."No." + '/' + FORMAT(TableTrfRcpLig_L."Line No.")), 1,
                    MAXSTRLEN(Result_L)));

                  }
                  // AFTER
                  //-->20230616-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003

                      //<--20160329-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-YPR-002
                    END ELSE BEGIN
                      //-->20230626-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003
                      // BEFORE
                      {

                        'Location', 'Code', TableRcpEnt_L."Transfer-from Code", FALSE, TRUE));

                      //<--20230626-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003
                      IF TxtTemp_L = '' THEN BEGIN
                        ErreurLigne_L := TRUE;
                        //-->20230616-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003

                          TableRcpEnt_L.FIELDCAPTION(TableRcpEnt_L."Transfer-from Code"),

                    }

                //<--20230626-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001

                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(TableRcpEnt_L."Item No.", 15, ' ');
                //-->20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
                // BEFORE

                //<--20140923-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-003
                //-->20150618-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-009
                // BEFORE
                {
                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 15, ' ');
                }
                // AFTER
                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(COPYSTR(TableRcpEnt_L."Trailers Registration", 1, 15), 15, ' ');
                //<--20150618-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-009

                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 15, ' ');
                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 1, ' ');
                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 1, ' ');
                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 15, ' ');
                // Code distributeur :
                //TODO-002 Code distributeur ? : Pour les apports
                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 15, ' ');
                //-->20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
                // BEFORE
                {
                IF TableRcpEnt_L."Weighing 1" < 0 THEN BEGIN
                }
                // AFTER
                IF (TableRcpEnt_L."Weighing 1" * w_Signe_L) < 0 THEN BEGIN
                  //<--20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
                  TxtEcrit_L += '-';
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  TxtEcrit_L += '0';
                END;
                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(FORMAT(ABS(TableRcpEnt_L."Weighing 1"), 0, '<Integer><Decimals><Comma,.>'), 11, ' ');

                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR('?', 12, ' '); // Poids total roulant
                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR('?', 12, ' '); // Poids surcharge
                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 15, ' ');
                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 15, ' ');

                //-->20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
                // BEFORE
                {
                IF TableRcpEnt_L."Weighing 2" < 0 THEN BEGIN
                }
                // AFTER
                IF (TableRcpEnt_L."Weighing 2" * w_Signe_L) < 0 THEN BEGIN
                  //<--20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
                  TxtEcrit_L += '-';
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  TxtEcrit_L += '0';
                END;
                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(FORMAT(ABS(TableRcpEnt_L."Weighing 2"), 0, '<Integer><Decimals><Comma,.>'), 11, ' ');

                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 15, ' ');
                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR('NAV', 3, ' ');
                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(TypeDoc_L, 20, ' ');
                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(TableRcpEnt_L."No.", 20, ' ');
                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 20, ' ');
                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 20, ' ');
                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 1, ' ');
                //-->20230612-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                // Campagne en fonction du param'trage
                IF booCampagneValeur_G THEN BEGIN
                  w_Campagne_L := TableRcpEnt_L."Campaign No.";
                  IF ToolsFile_G.TexteTrim(w_Campagne_L, 'T') = '' THEN BEGIN
                    w_Campagne_L := TableRcpEnt_L."Current Campaign No.";
                  END;
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  CLEAR(w_Campagne_L);
                END;
                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(w_Campagne_L, 4, ' ');
                //<--20230612-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                //-->20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                IF TableParameter_G."Export cahier pes'e" THEN BEGIN
                  w_Cahier_L := TableRcpEnt_L."Notebook Weighing No.";
                END;
                TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(w_Cahier_L, 30, ' ');       // Pour suivi des modifications & annulation
                //<--20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                //-->20230622-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL006
                END;  // A_Traiter
                IF ((NOT ErreurLigne_L) AND (NOT ATraiter_L)) THEN BEGIN
                  ErreurLigne_L := TRUE;
                END;
                //<--20230622-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL006

                IF NOT ErreurLigne_L THEN BEGIN
                  //-->20140930-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-006
                  //BEFORE
                  {
                  FluxBaseEcritTable (TableRcpEnt_L."Item No.", '', '', '', TableRcpEnt_L."Location Code", w_LocationCode_L, '', '');
                  }
                  //AFTER
                  //-->20230623-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                  // BEFORE
                  {
                  FluxBaseEcritTable (TableRcpEnt_L."Item No.", '', '', '', TableRcpEnt_L."Location Code", w_LocationCode_L, '', '', '');
                  }
                  // AFTER
                  IF (Fournisseur_L <> '') THEN BEGIN
                    FluxBaseEcritTable (TableRcpEnt_L."Item No.", Fournisseur_L, '', FournisseurCorr_L,
                      TableRcpEnt_L."Location Code", w_LocationCode_L, '', '', '', '');
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    FluxBaseEcritTable (TableRcpEnt_L."Item No.", '', '', '', TableRcpEnt_L."Location Code", w_LocationCode_L, '', '', ''
                      , '');
                  END;
                  //-->20230628-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                  IF w_LocationCodeFrom_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
                    FluxBaseEcritTable ('', '', '', '', TableRcpEnt_L."Transfer-from Code"
                      , w_LocationCodeFrom_L, '', '', '', '');
                  END;
                  //<--20230628-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                  //<--20230623-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                  //<--20140930-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-006

                  EcritFichier(TxtEcrit_L);

                  //-->20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
                  IF wTableTlcFluxModif_L THEN BEGIN
                    TableTlcFlux_G.ExportCancelDateTime := CURRENTDATETIME;
                    IF NOT TableTlcFlux_G.MODIFY(TRUE) THEN BEGIN
                      ToolsFile_G.LogWriteErreur(STRSUBSTNO(Text0009, TableTlcFlux_G.TABLECAPTION));
                    END;
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                  //<--20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
                    TableTlcFlux_G.Type  := TypeDoc_L;
                    TableTlcFlux_G."Document No." := TableRcpEnt_L."No.";
                    TableTlcFlux_G."Line No." := 0;
                    TableTlcFlux_G."sLine No." := 0;
                    TableTlcFlux_G.CreateDateTime := CURRENTDATETIME;

                      ToolsFile_G.LogWriteErreur(STRSUBSTNO(Text0009, TableTlcFlux_G.TABLECAPTION));
                    END;
                  //-->20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
                  END;
                  //<--20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
                END;
              END;
            //-->20150720-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-012
            END;
            //<--20150720-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-012
          END;
        UNTIL TableRcpEnt_L.NEXT=0;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE FluxVenteCereale@1000000008(DateDebutMvt_P@1000000009 : Date) Result_L : Text[256];
    VAR
      "<-- 3LI LOCALS -->"@1000000001 : Integer;
      TableVentEnt_L@1000000000 : Record 8053014;
      TableRcpEnt_L@1000000014 : Record 8052995;
      recOrdreCharg_L@1000000020 : Record 8053265;
      TxtEcrit_L@1000000008 : Text[1024];
      TxtTemp_L@1000000007 : Text[128];
      w_LocationCode_L@1000000002 : Text[30];
      w_Customer_L@1000000004 : Text[30];
      FormatFieldsManage_L@1000000003 : Codeunit 8060985;
      TypeDoc_L@1000000005 : Code[20];
      ErreurLigne_L@1000000006 : Boolean;
      A_Traiter_L@1000000010 : Boolean;
      w_Campagne_L@1000000011 : Text[10];
      TransporteurCorr_L@1000000013 : Text[128];
      wClientCorr_L@1000000012 : Text[128];
      Fournisseur_L@1000000015 : Code[20];
      FournisseurCorr_L@1000000016 : Text[128];
      w_SiteOrigine_L@1000000017 : Text[128];
      w_SiteDest_L@1000000018 : Text[128];
      CodeExpl_L@1000000019 : Code[10];
      Famille_L@1000000023 : Text[30];
      SousFamille_L@1000000022 : Text[30];
      TableItem_L@1000000021 : Record 27;
      wTableTlcFluxModif_L@1000000024 : Boolean;
      w_Cahier_L@1000000025 : Text[30];
      w_Signe_L@1000000026 : Decimal;
      TypeFlux_L@1000000027 : Text[3];
    BEGIN
      // Ventes c'r'ale.
      //============================================
      // Boucle sur ent^te 'xp'dition
      TypeDoc_L := 'T8053014';
      //-->20131121-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL
      {
      //BEFORE
      TableVentEnt_L.SETFILTER(TableVentEnt_L."Posting Date", '>= %1', CALCDATE('<-2M>', TODAY));
      }
      //AFTER
      TableVentEnt_L.SETFILTER(TableVentEnt_L."Posting Date", '>= %1', DateDebutMvt_P);
      //<--20131121-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL
      //-->20150720-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-011
      //-->20230704-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
      IF TableParameter_G."Apport Vente Retour" THEN BEGIN
        TableVentEnt_L.SETFILTER("Delivery Type", '%1|%2', TableVentEnt_L."Delivery Type"::Delivery
          , TableVentEnt_L."Delivery Type"::Resumption);
      END ELSE BEGIN
        //<--20230704-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
        TableVentEnt_L.SETRANGE("Delivery Type", TableVentEnt_L."Delivery Type"::Delivery);
        //-->20230704-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
      END;
      //<--20230704-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
      //-->20150918-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-015
      TableVentEnt_L.SETFILTER("Shipping Type", '<> %1', TableVentEnt_L."Shipping Type"::Customer);
      //<--20150918-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-015
      //-->20230710-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
      //-->20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
      // BEFORE
      {
      IF TableParameter_G."Apport Vte Annul'e non envoy'e" THEN BEGIN
        TableVentEnt_L.SETRANGE(Canceled, FALSE);
      END;
      }
      //<--20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
      //<--20230710-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002

      //<--20150720-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-011
      IF TableVentEnt_L.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          //-->20150918-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-015
          A_Traiter_L := FALSE;
          //<--20150918-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-015
          IF (TableVentEnt_L."Delivery Type" = TableVentEnt_L."Delivery Type"::Delivery) THEN BEGIN
          //-->20150918-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-015
            A_Traiter_L := TRUE;
            IF ((TableVentEnt_L."IC Partner Code" <> '') AND (TableVentEnt_L."IC Direction" = TableVentEnt_L."IC Direction"::Outgoing))
              THEN BEGIN
              A_Traiter_L := FALSE;
            END;
            //-->20230704-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF (TableVentEnt_L."Delivery Type" = TableVentEnt_L."Delivery Type"::Resumption) THEN BEGIN
              A_Traiter_L := TRUE;
            END;
          //-->20230704-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
          END;

          //-->20230626-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
          // Exclusion de certain client
          CLEAR(wClientCorr_L);
          //-->20230626-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003
          // Exclusion de certain magasin
          CLEAR(w_LocationCode_L);
          IF TableVentEnt_L."Location Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
            w_LocationCode_L := FormatFieldsManage_L.Ascii2Ansi(MoteurInterface_G.Getcorrespondance(GlobalTemplateCode_G,
              'Location', 'Code', TableVentEnt_L."Location Code", FALSE, TRUE));
            IF w_LocationCode_L = '$' THEN BEGIN
              A_Traiter_L := FALSE;
            END;
          END;
          //<--20230626-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003
          IF TableVentEnt_L."Customer No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
            wClientCorr_L := MoteurInterface_G.GetReferentialCorrespondance(CustomerTemplateCode_G, FALSE,
              TableVentEnt_L."Customer No.", TRUE);
            IF wClientCorr_L = '$' THEN BEGIN
              A_Traiter_L := FALSE;
            END;
          END;
          //<--20230626-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
          IF A_Traiter_L THEN BEGIN
          //<--20150918-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-015
            //-->20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
            // BEFORE
            {
            IF NOT TableTlcFlux_G.GET(TypeDoc_L, TableVentEnt_L."No.", 0, 0) THEN BEGIN
              //-->20230810-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
            END ELSE BEGIN
              A_Traiter_L := FALSE;
            END;
            }
            //<--20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
            // AFTER
            A_Traiter_L := FALSE;
            w_Signe_L := 1;
            IF NOT TableTlcFlux_G.GET(TypeDoc_L, TableVentEnt_L."No.", 0, 0) THEN BEGIN
              wTableTlcFluxModif_L := FALSE;
              // Uniquement si non annul'.
              IF ((TableParameter_G."Apport Vte Annul'e non envoy'e") AND (TableVentEnt_L.Canceled)) THEN BEGIN
              END ELSE BEGIN
                A_Traiter_L := TRUE;
              END;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              wTableTlcFluxModif_L := TRUE;
              // Pour ceux d'j. envoy' en cr'ation ne traiter que l'annulation si non d'j. envoy'e.
              IF ((TableParameter_G.ApporExportAnnulationFlux) AND (TableVentEnt_L.Canceled)
                AND (TableTlcFlux_G.ExportCancelDateTime = 0DT)) THEN BEGIN
                A_Traiter_L := TRUE;
                w_Signe_L := -1;
              END;
            END;

                TableVentEnt_L."Customer No.", TRUE);
              }
              // AFTER
              TxtTemp_L := wClientCorr_L;
              //<--//-->20230626-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
              IF (TxtTemp_L = '') THEN BEGIN
                IF STRLEN(TableVentEnt_L."Customer No.") < 6 THEN BEGIN
                  ErreurLigne_L := TRUE;
                  //-->20230616-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003
                  // BEFORE
                  {
                  ToolsFile_G.LogWriteInfo(COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(Text0013,
                    TableVentEnt_L.FIELDCAPTION(TableVentEnt_L."Customer No."),
                    TableVentEnt_L."Customer No.", TypeDoc_L + '/' + TableVentEnt_L."No."), 1, MAXSTRLEN(Result_L)));
                  }
                  // AFTER
                  ToolsFile_G.LogWriteInfo(COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(Text0013B
                    , TableVentEnt_L.FIELDCAPTION(TableVentEnt_L."Customer No.")
                    , TableVentEnt_L."Customer No."
                    , TypeDoc_L + '/' + TableVentEnt_L."No."
                    , CustomerTemplateCode_G, ''
                    ), 1, MAXSTRLEN(Result_L)));
                  //<--20230616-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  TxtTemp_L := TableVentEnt_L."Customer No.";
                END;
              END;
              TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(TxtTemp_L, 15, ' ');

              // Code distributeur
              //TODO-002 Code distributeur ? : Pour les apports
              TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 15, ' ');
              //-->20230710-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003
              // BEFORE
              {
              IF TableVentEnt_L."Weight out" < 0 THEN BEGIN
              }
              // AFTER
              //-->20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
              // BEFORE
              {
              IF ((TableVentEnt_L."Weight out" < 0)
                  OR ((TableParameter_G."Apport Annulation en n'gatif") AND (TableVentEnt_L."Cancellation Delivery"))) THEN BEGIN
              //-->20230710-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL003
              }
              // AFTER
              IF (((TableVentEnt_L."Weight out" * w_Signe_L) < 0)
                  OR ((TableParameter_G."Apport Annulation en n'gatif") AND (TableVentEnt_L."Cancellation Delivery"))) THEN BEGIN
                //<--20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
                TxtEcrit_L += '-';
              END ELSE BEGIN
                TxtEcrit_L += '0';
              END;
              TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(FORMAT(ABS(TableVentEnt_L."Weight out"), 0, '<Integer><Decimals><Comma,.>'), 11, ' ');

              TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR('?', 12, ' '); // Poids total roulant
              TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR('?', 12, ' '); // Poids surcharge
              TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 15, ' ');
              TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 15, ' ');

              IF TableVentEnt_L."Weighing 1" < 0 THEN BEGIN
                TxtEcrit_L += '-';
              END ELSE BEGIN
                TxtEcrit_L += '0';
              END;
              TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(FORMAT(ABS(TableVentEnt_L."Weighing 1"), 0, '<Integer><Decimals><Comma,.>'), 11, ' ');

              TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 15, ' ');
              TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR('NAV', 3, ' ');
              TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(TypeDoc_L, 20, ' ');
              TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(TableVentEnt_L."No.", 20, ' ');
              TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 20, ' ');
              TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 20, ' ');
              TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(' ', 1, ' ');
              //-->20230612-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
              // Campagne en fonction du param'trage
              IF booCampagneValeur_G THEN BEGIN
                w_Campagne_L := TableVentEnt_L."Campaign No.";
              END ELSE BEGIN
                CLEAR(w_Campagne_L);
              END;
              TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(w_Campagne_L, 4, ' ');
              //<--20230612-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
              //-->20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
              IF TableParameter_G."Export cahier pes'e" THEN BEGIN
                w_Cahier_L := TableVentEnt_L."Notebook Weighing No.";
              END;
              TxtEcrit_L += PADSTR(w_Cahier_L, 30, ' ');       // Pour suivi des modifications & annulation
              //<--20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
              //-->20230622-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL006
              END;  // A_Traiter
              IF ((NOT ErreurLigne_L) AND (NOT A_Traiter_L)) THEN BEGIN
                ErreurLigne_L := TRUE;
              END;
              //<--20230622-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL006

              IF NOT ErreurLigne_L THEN BEGIN
                //-->20140930-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-006
                //BEFORE
                {
                FluxBaseEcritTable (TableVentEnt_L."Item No.", '', '', '', TableVentEnt_L."Location Code", w_LocationCode_L,
                  TableVentEnt_L."Customer No.", w_Customer_L);
                }
                //AFTER
                //-->20230623-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                // BEFORE
                {
                FluxBaseEcritTable (TableVentEnt_L."Item No.", '', '', '', TableVentEnt_L."Location Code", w_LocationCode_L,
                  TableVentEnt_L."Customer No.", w_Customer_L, 'EXT');
                }
                // AFTER
                //-->20230628-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
                // BEFORE
                {

                  TableVentEnt_L."Customer No.", w_Customer_L, 'EXT', TableVentEnt_L."Ship-to Code");
                }

                //-->20230710-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL004
                // BEFORE
                {
                FluxBaseEcritTable (TableVentEnt_L."Item No.", Fournisseur_L, '', FournisseurCorr_L
                  , TableVentEnt_L."Location Code", w_LocationCode_L,
                  TableVentEnt_L."Customer No.", w_Customer_L, 'EXT', TableVentEnt_L."Ship-to Code");
                }
                // AFTER
                FluxBaseEcritTable (TableVentEnt_L."Item No.", Fournisseur_L, '', FournisseurCorr_L
                  , TableVentEnt_L."Location Code", w_LocationCode_L,
                  TableVentEnt_L."Customer No.", w_Customer_L, 'EXT', CodeExpl_L);

                //<--20230628-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
                //<--20230623-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
                //<--20140930-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-006

                EcritFichier(TxtEcrit_L);

                //-->20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
                IF wTableTlcFluxModif_L THEN BEGIN
                  TableTlcFlux_G.ExportCancelDateTime := CURRENTDATETIME;
                  IF NOT TableTlcFlux_G.MODIFY(TRUE) THEN BEGIN
                    ToolsFile_G.LogWriteErreur(STRSUBSTNO(Text0009, TableTlcFlux_G.TABLECAPTION));
                  END;
                END ELSE BEGIN
                //<--20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
                  TableTlcFlux_G.Type  := TypeDoc_L;
                  TableTlcFlux_G."Document No." := TableVentEnt_L."No.";
                  TableTlcFlux_G."Line No." := 0;
                  TableTlcFlux_G."sLine No." := 0;
                  TableTlcFlux_G.CreateDateTime := CURRENTDATETIME;
                  IF NOT TableTlcFlux_G.INSERT(TRUE) THEN BEGIN
                    ToolsFile_G.LogWriteErreur(STRSUBSTNO(Text0009, TableTlcFlux_G.TABLECAPTION));
                  END;
                //-->20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
                END;
                //<--20240123-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
              END;
            END;
          END;
        UNTIL TableVentEnt_L.NEXT=0;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE FluxBaseEcritTable@1000000009(Item_P@1000000000 : Code[20];Vendor_P@1000000002 : Code[20];ShipToAddress_P@1000000003 : Code[10];ShipToAddressTlc_P@1000000004 : Text[30];"Location Code_P"@1000000001 : Code[10];"Location Code TLC_P"@1000000005 : Text[30];"Sell-to Customer No._P"@1000000006 : Code[20];"Sell-to Customer No. TLC_P"@1000000007 : Text[30];"Sell-to Customer No. Type_P"@1000000008 : Text[10];"Customer ShipToAddress_P"@1000000009 : Code[10]);
    BEGIN
      // Ecrire une seule fois dans le fichier temporaire les articles, fournisseur . envoyer.
      IF Item_P <> '' THEN BEGIN
        IF NOT TableTlcFluxBase_G.GET(Item_P, '', '', '', '') THEN BEGIN
          CLEAR(TableTlcFluxBase_G);
          TableTlcFluxBase_G.Item := Item_P;
          IF TableTlcFluxBase_G.INSERT(TRUE) THEN;
        END;
      END;
      //-->20230626-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL004
      // BEFORE
      {
      IF ((Vendor_P <> '') AND (ShipToAddressTlc_P <> '')) THEN BEGIN
      }
      // AFTER
      IF ((Vendor_P <> '')
        AND ((ShipToAddressTlc_P <> '') OR (TableParameter_G."Site origine non obligatoire"))) THEN BEGIN
      //<--20230626-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL004
        IF NOT TableTlcFluxBase_G.GET('', Vendor_P, ShipToAddress_P, '', '') THEN BEGIN
          CLEAR(TableTlcFluxBase_G);
          TableTlcFluxBase_G.Vendor := Vendor_P;
          TableTlcFluxBase_G."Ship To Address" := ShipToAddress_P;
          TableTlcFluxBase_G.ShipToAddressTLC := ShipToAddressTlc_P;
          IF TableTlcFluxBase_G.INSERT(TRUE) THEN;
        END;
      END;
      IF ("Location Code TLC_P" <> '') THEN BEGIN
        IF NOT TableTlcFluxBase_G.GET('', '', '', "Location Code_P", '') THEN BEGIN
          CLEAR(TableTlcFluxBase_G);
          TableTlcFluxBase_G."Location Code" := "Location Code_P";
          TableTlcFluxBase_G."Location Code TLC" := "Location Code TLC_P";
          IF TableTlcFluxBase_G.INSERT(TRUE) THEN;
        END;
      END;
      IF ("Sell-to Customer No. TLC_P" <> '') THEN BEGIN
        //-->20140930-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-006
        //BEFORE
        {
        IF NOT TableTlcFluxBase_G.GET('', '', '', '', "Sell-to Customer No._P") THEN BEGIN
        }
        //AFTER
        //-->20230623-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
        // BEFORE
        {
        IF NOT TableTlcFluxBase_G.GET('', '', '', '', "Sell-to Customer No._P", "Sell-to Customer No. Type_P") THEN BEGIN
        }
        // AFTER
        IF NOT TableTlcFluxBase_G.GET('', '', "Customer ShipToAddress_P", ''
          , "Sell-to Customer No._P", "Sell-to Customer No. Type_P") THEN BEGIN
        //<--20230623-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
        //<--20140930-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-006
          CLEAR(TableTlcFluxBase_G);
          TableTlcFluxBase_G."Sell-to Customer No." := "Sell-to Customer No._P";
          //-->20230623-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
          TableTlcFluxBase_G."Ship To Address" := "Customer ShipToAddress_P";
          //<--20230623-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
          TableTlcFluxBase_G."Sell-to Customer No. TLC" := "Sell-to Customer No. TLC_P";
          //-->20140930-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-006
          TableTlcFluxBase_G."Sell-to Customer No. Type" := "Sell-to Customer No. Type_P";
          //<--20140930-INT14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL-006
          IF TableTlcFluxBase_G.INSERT(TRUE) THEN;
        END;
      END;
    END;

      wFax_L@1000000033 : Text[30];
    BEGIN
      IF TableTlcFluxBase_G.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
        FileNameArtiOuvert_G := FALSE;
        REPEAT
          IF TableTlcFluxBase_G.Item <> '' THEN BEGIN
            IF TableItem_L.GET(TableTlcFluxBase_G.Item) THEN BEGIN
              ErreurLigne_L := FALSE;

              SousFamille_L := FormatFieldsManage_L.Ascii2Ansi(MoteurInterface_G.Getcorrespondance(GlobalTemplateCode_G,
                'CMC Sub-Family', 'Code', TableItem_L."Sub-Family Code", FALSE, TRUE));
              IF SousFamille_L = '' THEN BEGIN

                    END ELSE BEGIN
                      ConvKg_L := 1;

                    IF TableItem_L.Density <> 0 THEN BEGIN
                      ConvKg_L := ConvKg_L * TableItem_L.Density;
                    END;
                  END;
                END;
              END;
              IF ConvKg_L = 0 THEN BEGIN
                ErreurLigne_L := TRUE;
                ToolsFile_G.LogWriteInfo(COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(Text0007, TableItem_L."No."), 1, 256));
              END;

    PROCEDURE EcritFichier@1000000002(Texte_P@1000000000 : Text[1024]);
    VAR
      "<-- 3LI LOCALS -->"@1000000002 : Integer;
      FileN_L@1000000001 : Text[256];
      TxtMessage_L@1000000003 : Text[256];
    BEGIN
      IF NOT FileNameOuvert_G THEN BEGIN
        ToolsFile_G.LogInitFileExecute(FileName_G);

        ToolsFile_G.SplitFilename(FileName_G, FilePath_G, FileN_L);
        FileTemp_G := FilePath_G + 'Temp_' + FileN_L;
        TxtMessage_L := ToolsFile_G.FileCreateNotExist(FileName_G, 'Temp_', FileExport_G);

        IF TxtMessage_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          ToolsFile_G.LogWriteErreur(COPYSTR(TxtMessage_L, 1, 256));
          CLEARLASTERROR;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          FileExport_G.CREATEOUTSTREAM(FileExportOutstream_G);
          FileNameOuvert_G := TRUE;
        END;
      END;
      IF FileNameOuvert_G THEN BEGIN
        FileExportOutstream_G.WRITETEXT(Texte_P);
        FileExportOutstream_G.WRITETEXT();
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE EcritFichierDivers@1000000011(Texte_P@1000000000 : Text[1024];VAR FileExport_P@1000000005 : File;VAR FileExportOutstream_P@1000000004 : OutStream;VAR FileName_P@1000000006 : Text[256];VAR FileNameOuvert_P@1000000007 : Boolean);
    VAR
      "<-- 3LI LOCALS -->"@1000000002 : Integer;
      FileN_L@1000000001 : Text[256];
      TxtMessage_L@1000000003 : Text[256];
    BEGIN
      IF NOT FileNameOuvert_P THEN BEGIN
        TxtMessage_L := ToolsFile_G.FileCreateNotExist(FileName_P, 'Temp_', FileExport_P);

        IF TxtMessage_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          ToolsFile_G.LogWriteErreur(COPYSTR(TxtMessage_L, 1, 256));
          CLEARLASTERROR;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          FileExport_P.CREATEOUTSTREAM(FileExportOutstream_P);
          FileNameOuvert_P := TRUE;
        END;
      END;
      IF FileNameOuvert_P THEN BEGIN
        FileExportOutstream_P.WRITETEXT(Texte_P);
        FileExportOutstream_P.WRITETEXT();
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE "-- ADV FCT --"@1000000012();
    BEGIN
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetQtyFromTrsReceiptLine@1000000013(TrsReceiptLine@1000000000 : Record 5747;VAR Sign@1000000002 : Text[30]) ReturnValue : Decimal;
    VAR
      ItemLedgerEntry@1000000001 : Record 32;
    BEGIN
      ReturnValue := 0;
      Sign := '0';
      // Get item Ledger Entry
      ItemLedgerEntry.SETRANGE("Item No.", TrsReceiptLine."Item No.");
      ItemLedgerEntry.SETRANGE("Entry Type", ItemLedgerEntry."Entry Type"::Transfer);
      ItemLedgerEntry.SETRANGE("Document No.", TrsReceiptLine."Document No.");
      ItemLedgerEntry.SETRANGE("Location Code", TrsReceiptLine."Transfer-to Code");
      IF (TrsReceiptLine."Variant Code" <> '') THEN BEGIN
        ItemLedgerEntry.SETRANGE("Variant Code", TrsReceiptLine."Variant Code");
      END;
      IF (ItemLedgerEntry.FINDFIRST()) THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          ReturnValue += ItemLedgerEntry.Quantity;
        UNTIL(ItemLedgerEntry.NEXT()=0);
      END;
      IF (ReturnValue < 0) THEN
        Sign := '-';

      //-->20230710-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetBonLiv1Recep@1000000014(P_RecReceiptLine@1000000000 : Record 121;VAR P_RecSalesShipLine@1000000001 : Record 111;VAR P_RecTrfRcpLine_L@1000000013 : Record 5747;p_RechEcrArt@1000000009 : Boolean) p_Trouve : Boolean;
    VAR
      recAffretement_L@1000000002 : Record 60013;
      recOrdreCharg_L@1000000003 : Record 8053265;
      recSalesShipLine_L@1000000004 : Record 111;
      recTrfRcpLine_L@1000000010 : Record 5747;
      BL_NO_L@1000000005 : Code[20];
      BL_No_Line_L@1000000006 : Integer;
      TR_NO_L@1000000012 : Code[20];
      TR_No_Line_L@1000000011 : Integer;
      P_SortirPlus1_L@1000000007 : Boolean;
      LitNext_L@1000000008 : Integer;
      recTlcExport_L@1000000014 : Record 8053415;
      wEcart_L@1000000019 : Integer;
      wEcartSel_L@1000000020 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // Si un seul BL ==> Retourner l'enregistrement.
      CLEAR(MsgBonLiv1Recep_G);
      CLEAR(BL_NO_L);
      CLEAR(BL_No_Line_L);
      p_Trouve := FALSE;
      P_SortirPlus1_L := FALSE;
      //-->20230915-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL002
      CLEAR(TR_NO_L);
      CLEAR(TR_No_Line_L);
      MsgBonLiv1Recep_G := STRSUBSTNO('%1_', p_RechEcrArt);
      IF (NOT P_SortirPlus1_L) AND ((BL_NO_L <> '') OR (TR_NO_L <> '')) THEN BEGIN
        // Si pas plusieurs ==> On a trouv' Ne pas chercher plus loin
      END ELSE BEGIN

    PROCEDURE MessageDebug@1000000010(Message_P@1000000000 : Text[250]);
    BEGIN
      IF ((USERID = 'ERIC SAINT-LEGER') OR (USERID = 'CHRISTOPHE DAL')) THEN BEGIN
        IF MessageDebugNb_G < 20 THEN BEGIN
          MessageDebugNb_G += 1;
          MESSAGE(Message_P);
        END;
      END;

      //-->20231002-Int14-CMC02.02.00-ADVITAM-CDL001
    END;

      21/08/2017 - YPR011 : TC33669 Ecart tonnage entre Navagri et TLC


      Remarques :
      1 - Mode Livraison TLC : "Shipment Method Code" : suivant type de condition livraion (Table 10)
          T111  ou T110  (Ventes)                         : Delivery ==> '3', Removal ==> '1'
          T6661 ou T6660 (Ventes retour)                  : Delivery ==> '1', Removal ==> '3'
          T121  ou T120  (Achat fournisseur : R'ception)  : Delivery ==> '1', Removal ==> '3'
          T6651 ou T6650 (Achat fournisseur : Retour)     : Delivery ==> '3', Removal ==> '1'
          T5746          (Transfert r'ception)            : Delivery ==> '1', Removal ==> '3'
      2 - si Mode Livraison TLC = '3' ==> Transporteur obligatoire.
